    ✔ no application role can UPDATE, DELETE or TRUNCATE a receipt; app_worker cannot UPDATE an anchor (37.372202ms)
    ✔ a receipt cannot name another organisation than its anchor, and PUBLIC and app_owner hold no right on the table (12.541072ms)
  ▶ audit.archive_anchors
    ✔ archives every anchor once: key, version and SHA-256 are on the receipt; a second run puts nothing (6.345021ms)
    ✔ an object-store error leaves the anchor for the next run and the lag grows; the retry archives it (11.453809ms)
    ✔ 412 with an equal body inserts a receipt with the existing version (crash between put and insert) (9.291417ms)
    ✔ 412 with a different body publishes audit.anchor_mismatch (archive_differs) and inserts nothing (24.547688ms)
    ✔ the task emits AuditAnchorArchiveLagSeconds on every run, 0 when nothing waits (5.330896ms)
  ✔ audit.archive_anchors (57.601386ms)
  ▶ audit.verify_anchors: the archive is the authority
    ✔ untouched data verifies clean with the archive, and the archive was actually read (13.261886ms)
    ✔ tamper: an event row changed and the anchor chain rewritten consistently as owner -> db_differs_from_archive and an alert (90.033336ms)
    ✔ an anchor missing from the database but present in the archive is db_differs_from_archive, unless it is after the restore point (22.225635ms)
    ✔ a deleted receipt is not_archived once the anchor is older than the grace; a fresh anchor is not flagged under the default grace (24.757341ms)
    ✔ a receipt that names a version the archive does not list is not_archived (forged receipt) (13.625328ms)
    ✔ a later version of an archived key is itself a finding (archive_extra_version), judged on the earliest version (11.40627ms)
    ✔ an unreadable archive fails the task after the database checks and the mismatch events; it never reads as clean (15.405142ms)
  ✔ audit.verify_anchors: the archive is the authority (191.311097ms)

▶ notifications on PostgreSQL 17 (BE-06, worker side)
  ▶ the send job
    ✔ e-mail: renders the published version in the person's language, attaches the .ics, stores the vendor reference and no address or text (46.437381ms)
    ✔ language: the person's language when published, else the default; SMS and push render without a subject/with a title (41.325018ms)
    ✔ TC-NT-08: SMS off and e-mail on - the SMS is suppressed with its reason and the provider is never called; e-mail is sent (24.347532ms)
    ✔ suppression reasons: no address, unknown recipient, inactive patient, no contributor, no template (42.373978ms)
    ✔ in-app notices need no vendor and are sent even when every external channel is off (TC-NT-04) (5.381931ms)
    ✔ TC-NT-04: a failing provider is retried by the queue; the failure is an error CLASS; the last attempt publishes delivery_failed; recovery sends with the same token (37.427044ms)
    ✔ a vendor refusal (4xx) is final: failed, no queue retry, delivery_failed published (11.670626ms)
    ✔ quiet hours defer the send to when they end, restore the attempt count and queue the job (26.483083ms)
    ✔ a template that leaks text is stopped at send time by the output scan; the provider is never called (8.066996ms)
    ✔ a render error (a declared value missing) is a final failure, not a retry (7.37739ms)
    ✔ BE06-F1: a merge that re-points the row during the vendor call - sent, failed and deferred outcomes are still recorded (under the survivor) (34.438108ms)
    ✔ a patient merged while the job waited: the job follows the survivor and sends to the survivor's contacts (on_merged = resolve) (10.829786ms)
    ✔ a staff notice runs under the organisation scope and resolves through the principal contributor (7.826323ms)
    ✔ no address, message text or canary reaches a log line (0.196289ms)
  ✔ the send job (305.694548ms)
  ▶ delivery receipts (33.1 notifications.on_receipt, SBR-16)
    ✔ a receipt for a kept notification of a merged patient attaches to the SURVIVOR; a repeat of the event is a no-op (17.356367ms)
    ✔ untrusted payloads: unknown reference, bad shape, bad status, oversized and ambiguous references record nothing (14.291509ms)
    ✔ the inbound handler records through the same path (2.642204ms)
    ✔ the worker role cannot update or delete a receipt, or write one for a patient outside its job scope (4.361394ms)
  ✔ delivery receipts (33.1 notifications.on_receipt, SBR-16) (41.165017ms)
  ▶ channel adapters implement the BE-02 interface
    ✔ argument checks reject header injection, malformed addresses and oversize text without echoing them (0.69226ms)
    ✔ provider errors map to AdapterFailure with the provider's retryability; any other exception is retryable; health never throws (0.402386ms)
  ✔ channel adapters implement the BE-02 interface (1.221424ms)
